### GPU telemetry during the isolated GEMM runs

NVML before and after every (projection, T) block (its mean power from the energy counter, and the power-cap violation time); the SM clock and the samples with the software power cap active from the 100 ms nvidia-smi sampler ("busy": samples above 100 W).

| model | (projection, T) blocks | seconds | SM clock min / median / max, MHz | busy SM clock median, MHz | block mean power median / max, W | sampled power max, W | power cap | power limit |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|---:|
| Llama-3.1-8B | 49 | 98 | 180 / 2640 / 2835 | 2655 | 131 / 330 | 340 | 0.0 ms in 0 of 49 blocks; 30 of 1332 samples | 500 W |
| Mistral-7B-v0.3 | 49 | 100 | 172 / 2640 / 2835 | 2655 | 130 / 289 | 333 | 0.0 ms in 0 of 49 blocks; 25 of 1409 samples | 500 W |
| Phi-4 | 28 | 61 | 172 / 2295 / 2835 | 2632 | 156 / 439 | 487 | 2873.2 ms in 3 of 28 blocks; 55 of 1385 samples | 500 W |
| Qwen3.8-27B | 84 | 171 | 172 / 2602 / 2835 | 2647 | 135 / 356 | 388 | 609.9 ms in 2 of 84 blocks; 70 of 3200 samples | 500 W |

The power limit is 500 W (the default is 600 W); changing it needs root, so it was recorded, not changed.
